| 14 | Release and packaged helpers default to the real `%LOCALAPPDATA%\CITAR` and several gates run them (minor) | `crates/citar-helper/src/home.rs:95-125` | `gate_run.py helper` refuses a run without `--home` under the package's target; each such gate names `--home`; CI runs pass `--home` too (P5.4.1, P5.18.1 rule 2) | 5-03, 5-07, 5-13 |
| 15 | The job host's `-m` puts the state directory on `sys.path` (minor) | `citar/jobs/host.py:86` | `python -P -m citar.jobs host`, with a test that a module planted in the working directory is not imported (P5.6, P5.13.1) | 5-05 |
| 16 | The bundle's build can analyse the checkout, citar.iss cannot find a bundle outside `dist/`, and its Python notices list the wrong packages (minor) | `installer/citar.spec:28,84,96`; `installer/citar.iss:73` | PyInstaller with `-P` from outside the checkout and an absolute spec path; `/DBundleDir=`; `PYTHON-LICENSES.txt` from the `citar[all]` closure plus CPython's and the bootloader's licences (P5.5, P5.6) | 5-05 |
| 17 | The staged release's doctor runs against the live state while the old release serves (minor) | P5.13.3's old step 4 | Step 3 runs it with every state setting in a temporary folder, so nothing new touches the live database before the switch; a cutover now verifies before its switch as well (P5.13.3) | 5-10b |
| 18 | `release_checksums.py --verify` and the image check have no dry-run form (minor) | P5.9, P5.15 | `--verify --base-url --pypi-json`, rehearsed by 5-13; RELEASING.md names `podman run` in WSL (P5.9, P5.15) | 5-08, 5-09, 5-13 |
| 19 | 5-02 is too large; 5-10 carries three server docs (minor) | Appendix F | 5-02 split into 5-02a and 5-02b as proposed; 5-10's docs moved to 5-12; and 5-10 is split again into 5-10a and 5-10b, because findings 6, 7, 8 and 17 add to it | Appendix F |
| 20 | winget may be absent, Scoop refuses an elevated shell, `brew audit` on loopback URLs (minor) | P5.9 | JSON-schema validation is the gate and `winget validate`/`install` run where present, with a notice otherwise; Scoop with `-RunAsAdmin`; audit and style on the real-URL formula, install and test on the loopback copy (P5.9) | 5-08 |
| 21 | A free-threaded Python gets pip's bare error (minor) | P5.8 | The platform check reads `Py_GIL_DISABLED` and gives the plain message, tested through the hook (P5.8) | 5-07 |

---

## Appendix F: Phase 5 work breakdown

Each package is sized for one implementation session (a Phase 4 package or smaller), starts from the previous package's merge, and ends with its gates passing, then a review and a fix round. The standard gates S1-S12 (P5.18.5) are named where they apply; every other gate is local and can be rerun by a reviewer, and CI-only gates are named as such, with the agent that proves them (P5.18.2). Every package keeps the safety rules of P5.18.1, builds its inputs from its own head (P5.18.3), and runs release helpers and Windows servers only through `scripts/ci/gate_run.py` once 5-03 has made it.

### 5-01: Versions, the build label and `release.py`

- **Depends on:** nothing (`v0.1.6` at `51ce329`)
- **Estimated size:** ~450 Python (`scripts/release.py`) + ~350 tests + small edits
- **Scope:** P5.2: `scripts/release.py` (`check`, `bump`, `prepare`, `label`, `meta`, `notes`, `assets`) over the checked set (Cargo.toml, the lock's eleven members, the fuzz lock's three, `citar/__init__.py`, `installer/citar.iss`'s `AppVersion` with a new `AppVersionNumeric`, KNOWN_ISSUES' header, the CHANGELOG); `citar.iss`'s comment and `docs/INSTALL.md:33` made version-free; `tests/test_release_script.py`; test.yml's lint job runs `release.py check`; CONTRIBUTING's "Releasing" names `release.py bump` (RELEASING.md is 5-09's); any test that pins the version, found by gate 5, fixed.
- **Gates:**
  1. `release.py check` passes on the tree; each place planted out of step, one at a time, fails naming its file and line.
  2. In a scratch clone (`C:/dev/target/citar-p-5-01/clone`): `bump 0.1.6 --date 2026-11-01` moves every place and nothing else (`git diff --stat` recorded); `cargo metadata --locked` accepts the lock, and rust.yml's `cargo check --locked` of the fuzz crate accepts its lock; `cargo xtask check` passes; `check --version 0.1.6` passes; the CHANGELOG has `## [0.1.6] - 2026-11-01`, a new empty Unreleased and both compare links; a second bump to 0.1.6 and a bump to 0.1.4 are refused.
  3. `prepare`: `--mode tag --version 0.1.5` passes at the head and `--version 0.1.6` fails; `--mode dry-run --version 0.1.6` bumps the scratch clone and passes; `--mode ci` only checks.
  4. `notes 0.1.5` equals what release.yml's awk prints from the same file, byte for byte; `label` and `meta` for each mode (a bad version, `0.1.6;rm`, refused); `assets 0.1.6` and `assets 0.1.6-rc.1` equal P5.10's list (PEP 440 names `0.1.6rc1`).
  5. **The whole suite runs bumped, as every dry run will run it:** in the scratch clone after the bump, with `cargo xtask develop` into the package's target (the dev-guard helpers built, so the update end-to-end tests run rather than skip) and the venv `C:/dev/venv/p-5-01-bumped`: the whole S1 suite, its count and skips equal to S1's at the head; S7's nextest over the workspace (the `jobs` golden set: the build id moved, no bless) and `cargo golden check`.
  6. S1, S1b, S2-S4, S8, S10, S11.

### 5-02a: The workflow guard, the pins and test.yml's call

- **Depends on:** 5-01
- **Estimated size:** ~450 Python (`scripts/ci/check_workflows.py`) + ~350 tests and fixtures + ~150 workflow YAML (about 120 pinned references, test.yml, release.yml's gate)
- **Scope:** P5.17's `check_workflows.py`, rules 1 to 15 (rule 16 is 5-09's), and `tests/test_workflows.py` with a planted fixture per rule; every remote `uses:` in the seven workflows pinned to its commit SHA with a version comment (each tag resolved read-only with `gh api`, annotated tags dereferenced to their commit); **release.yml reduced to its gate** (one job that fails with the Phase 5 notice: no build, no publishing job, no environment, until 5-09), with `helper.test.mjs` reading helper.yml's `artifact` matrix in place of release.yml's `build-helper`; test.yml's `workflow_call` inputs, `release.py prepare` in every job, the concurrency group, the Linux 3.14 leg, and lint running `check_workflows.py`; docs.yml's pins only.
- **Gates:**
  1. `check_workflows.py` passes on the tree; each planted fixture fails naming its job and rule: a publishing step without the guard; the guard without the event check; a guard under `always()`; docs.yml's wiki step without its main guard; an environment named in test.yml; a literal `environment: helper-signing` in release.yml; a called workflow's group without `github.workflow`; `${{ inputs.version }}` in a `run:`; a maturin step without the pin; a build before `prepare`; an upload of `*.key`; `build_wiki.py --push` in a workflow; an action referenced by tag; `secrets: inherit` on a calling job; an unnamed download and a `pattern: helper-*` download in a package.yml fixture; an upload named `pkg-x` in test.yml; `cargo build` in a step that reads a secret; `pip install` in a release.yml job holding `id-token: write`.
  2. Every pin resolves: for each reference, `gh api` confirms the SHA is a commit of that repository and the commented tag points at it (read-only; the list recorded).
  3. Every workflow parses; `cargo xtask check` passes; `node --test citar/web/tests/helper.test.mjs` passes reading helper.yml, and fails with a sixth name planted.
  4. **S1c:** the suite on Windows' Python 3.14 passes before the CI leg is added. Tripwire: a 3.14 failure that needs more than small fixes is recorded in KNOWN_ISSUES with its tests named and skipped on 3.14 only, rather than growing the package.
  5. **CI-only, at the merge push (PR #5's runs, watched by the merge agent):** test.yml's 3.14 leg and every other workflow green on the pinned actions.
  6. S1, S1b, S2-S4, S8, S10, S11.

### 5-03: The helper's release builds and the signing path

- **Depends on:** 5-02b
- **Estimated size:** ~200 Rust (`cargo xtask verify-manifest` and its tests) + ~250 workflow YAML + ~300 Python (`verify_manifest.py`, `gate_run.py`, tests) + the names test
- **Scope:** P5.4: package.yml's `helpers` matrix (the self-updating build under its unversioned name and the packaged build in its versioned archive; the Linux builds in the manylinux_2_28 containers with the `objdump -T` glibc check; each run with `--version`, `--self-test` and `--home`; the packaged one's `update check`; the Fedora TLS check; the macOS deployment target; the label) and `helper-manifest` job (manifest, throwaway key in `$RUNNER_TEMP`, signature, both verifiers, the one-byte negative, the `update-keys.pub` check, and the artifacts `pkg-helper-manifest`, `pkg-helper-manifest-dry-run` and `pkg-xtask-linux-x64`); helper.yml's Linux `artifact` builds moved into the same containers; `xtask verify-manifest`; `scripts/ci/verify_manifest.py`; **`scripts/ci/gate_run.py`** with its `helper` and `server` modes (P5.18.1 rules 2 and 11); `helper.test.mjs` reads package.yml's `helpers` matrix and helper.yml's `artifact`; check_workflows rule 9 covers the key folder; the archives carry `LICENSE` and `NOTICE.md` (5-04 adds the third file).
- **Gates:**
  1. **Windows:** both builds of `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc` in `release` with the label, run only through `gate_run.py helper` with `--home` under the target: `--version` names V, the label and the target; `--self-test` passes; the packaged build's `update check` says packaged and `notify`, and `updates = "auto"` in its `helper.json` is refused; `cargo nextest run -p citar-helper --features packaged` passes. `gate_run.py` refuses a helper run without `--home` or with one outside the target, and a server without `CITAR_HOST=127.0.0.1` or on port 8799 (its tests).
  2. **WSL:** both linux-x64 builds in the manylinux_2_28 container under podman; `objdump -T` shows no `GLIBC_` version above 2.28, and **a gate that can fail:** the same check refuses a build made on WSL's own Ubuntu 24.04 (glibc 2.39); the archive's listing is exactly its files (three until 5-04); `--self-test` on WSL's glibc.
  3. **Signing, in WSL:** with the linux-x64 build and stand-in copies for the other four names, `helper-manifest` writes the manifest; a key from `keygen` in `/tmp`; `sign-manifest`; `verify-manifest` passes with its public key, fails with another key and on a manifest with one byte changed; `verify_manifest.py` agrees in all three; the release build (no test features) with the empty `update-keys.pub` reports updates unavailable.
  4. **Names:** `helper.test.mjs` passes reading package.yml; a sixth name planted in the matrix, or a matrix line out of step with helper.yml, fails.
  5. `check_workflows.py`: package.yml names no environment or secret; an upload path under `$RUNNER_TEMP/throwaway` is refused.
  6. **CI-only, at the merge push:** the ten builds on five runners, the glibc check on both Linux runners, the `helper-manifest` job, helper.yml's container builds.
  7. S1, S2-S4, S6, S7, S8, S9 (gates 2-3), S10 (helper.json and the machine key unchanged), S11, S12.

### 5-07: The install scripts: wheels only, the helper download

- **Depends on:** 5-06
- **Estimated size:** ~250 bash + ~200 PowerShell + ~180 workflow YAML + ~250 tests
- **Scope:** P5.8: `install.sh` and `install.ps1` (`--only-binary=citar`, the platform check with its free-threaded case and its test hook, `--prefix`/`-Prefix`, `--bin-dir`/`-BinDir`, `--no-profile`, `-NoPath`, `--from`/`-From` with a file or folder, `--worker`/`-Worker` downloading the self-updating helper with its checksum, `CITAR_RELEASE_URL` with the loopback-only `http` rule, uninstall within the given prefix); package.yml's `install` matrix (ubuntu x64 and arm64, macOS arm64 and x64, Windows) against the built wheels and helpers on a loopback server; `gate_run.py install-ps1`.
- **Gates:**
  1. **WSL, as `claude`, everything in a temporary `HOME`:** `install.sh --from <manylinux wheel built from this head> --prefix … --bin-dir … --no-profile --no-setup` installs a working `citar`; a folder holding only an sdist is refused with the no-wheel message; the platform check's message for musl, an unknown architecture, glibc 2.27 and a free-threaded Python (through its test hook); `--worker` with `CITAR_RELEASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:<port>` serving the WSL helper build (from the container, this head) and its `.sha256` installs a helper that runs (`--version --home <temp>`), a wrong checksum is refused before anything is written, and an `http` URL to a non-loopback host is refused; `--uninstall` leaves the prefix and bin folder empty.
  2. **Windows, only through `gate_run.py install-ps1`:** `install.ps1 -Prefix … -BinDir … -NoPath -From <win wheel built from this head> -NoSetup` installs a working `citar`; `-Worker` against a loopback server installs the Windows helper, run through `gate_run.py helper`; the ARM64 and free-threaded messages through the hook; the user's PATH value is byte-identical before and after; `-Uninstall` with the same prefix removes only it; the wrapper refuses a run without `-Prefix` and `-BinDir` under the target or without `-NoPath`.
  3. **CI-only, at the merge push:** the `install` matrix on five platforms (PATH changes allowed on runners).
  4. S1 (the tests), S2, S4, S8, S9 (gate 1), S10, S11.

### 5-08: Package managers and assembling a release

- **Depends on:** 5-07
- **Estimated size:** ~500 Python (`release_checksums.py` with `--verify`'s dry-run form, `assemble_release.py`) + the templates + ~250 workflow YAML + ~300 tests
- **Scope:** P5.9 and P5.10: `packaging/` templates (Scoop `citar` and `citar-helper`, winget `game/` and `helper/`, Homebrew `citar-helper.rb` with its service block), `packaging/homebrew/citar.rb` deleted; `scripts/release_checksums.py` rewritten (`--from-dir`, `--base-url`, `--out`, `--write`, `--verify` with `--pypi-json`); `scripts/ci/assemble_release.py` and package.yml's `assemble` job (the list, one build across every artifact with the sdist build's `unknown` label, one result hash, `twine check`, `SHA256SUMS`, the notes, the subject list, the manifests, the three artifacts); the `managers` job (Scoop with `-RunAsAdmin`, the winget schema validation and `winget` where present, Homebrew's audit on the real URLs and install on the loopback copy, on macOS arm64, macOS x64 and Linux x64); `packaging/README.md` rewritten.

  1. `assemble_release.py` on a fake artifact tree: the complete set passes and writes `SHA256SUMS` that `sha256sum -c` accepts; a missing asset, an extra one, a helper reporting another build, a wheel reporting another jobs build, a wheel with another result hash, and an sdist build with a label other than `unknown` each fail naming the file; the sdist build's `unknown` is not compared with L.
  2. The generator over the package's local artifacts (stand-ins for the CI-only files) writes every manifest: each URL and hash right; the Scoop JSON has its keys and parses; each winget file starts with its schema header, has the required fields (`Documentations` spelled so) and validates against its schema with `jsonschema`; the formula has a URL and SHA-256 under each of the four platform blocks, and `ruby -c` accepts it in WSL (ruby installed as root, recorded).
  3. `release_checksums.py --verify V --base-url http://127.0.0.1:<port> --pypi-json FILE` over the fake release passes; one changed byte in an asset and a PyPI hash that differs each fail naming the file.
  4. **CI-only, at the merge push:** `assemble` and `managers`: `scoop install` of both packages from loopback, the schema validation, `winget validate` and `install --manifest` where present (else the notice), `brew audit --strict` and `brew style` on the real-URL formula, `brew install` and `brew test` on the loopback copy, on three platforms.
  5. S1 (the tests), S2, S4, S8, S9 (gate 2's ruby), S10, S11.

- **Scope:** P5.11, P5.12, P5.15: release.yml written (the gate removed; `meta`; `tests` and `package` with no `secrets:` and `contents: read`; the tag-only `sign-helper-manifest` with the tag-expression environment, building nothing, running the prebuilt xtask through `release_sign.py` on the runner's Python, `persist-credentials: false`; `check-signature`; `attest-files`; `publish-pypi`; `publish-image` (`:V-<arch>`, `:V`); `attest-image`; `github-release` as a draft then published; `image-tags`; the dry-run-only `dry-run-summary`; concurrency; per-job permissions); check_workflows rule 16; `packaging/RELEASING.md` (P5.15, with the re-run table) and `scripts/release/managers.sh` (clones into a temporary folder, copies, shows the diff, pushes only with `--push`); CONTRIBUTING's "Releasing".

If the run fails at startup on the empty environment name, P5.11's fallback (`release-dry-run.yml`) is made within those rounds.

### 5-10a: The server's release layout: units, backup, wrappers

- **Depends on:** 5-09
- **Estimated size:** ~250 bash (`citar-backup.sh`, `citar-admin.sh`) + ~150 Python (the wizard's unit, the retirement) + units + ~250 tests
- **Scope:** P5.13.1 and P5.13.2: `deploy/citar.service` (the console script, `WorkingDirectory=/var/lib/citar`, `CPUWeight=50` and `IOWeight=50` to start, the `MemoryHigh` rule recorded beside the limits), `citar-backup.sh` (the whole state directory and the release that wrote it), `citar-admin.sh` (`systemd-run` as `citar` with the sandbox, in `$CITAR_DEPLOY_SLICE` when set), `citar-secrets.sh` in substance unchanged; the wizard's unit rendered from `deploy/citar.service`'s directives (a test holds them equal); `install.sh --server` on the layout, with `--plan`; `requirements.txt`, `scripts/sync_requirements.py` and test.yml's step retired (code references updated; the docs are 5-12's, listed in the as-built note).
- **Gates:**
  1. `shellcheck` on every script in `deploy/` (installed in WSL as root, recorded); `systemd-analyze verify` of the units in WSL.
  2. `citar-backup.sh` in WSL against a temporary state directory: a file planted in each subfolder is in the tarball, the live database files are not, the snapshot passes its integrity check and names the release; a corrupted database makes the backup `UNTRUSTWORTHY`.
  3. The wizard's rendered unit and `deploy/citar.service` hold the same `[Service]` directives; `install.sh --server --plan` names the layout; `citar-admin` prints its `systemd-run` command with the slice when `CITAR_DEPLOY_SLICE` is set and without it otherwise.
  4. S1, S1b, S2 (ruff alone from here), S3, S4, S8 (test.yml), S9 (gates 1-2), S10, S11.

### 5-10b: `citar-deploy` and the deploy bundle

- **Depends on:** 5-10a
- **Estimated size:** ~500 bash (`citar-deploy.sh`) + ~200 Python (`scripts/deploy/bundle.py`) + ~300 tests + the expected plans
- **Scope:** P5.13.3 and P5.13.5: `deploy/citar-deploy.sh` (`install`, `cutover`, `rollback`, `uncutover`, `status`, `prune`, `--plan`, `--slice`/`CITAR_DEPLOY_SLICE`; the staged verification with temporary state; the revision recorded, the service stopped and the snapshot taken just before the switch; the units and scripts installed; the automatic rollback that restores the snapshot when the revision moved; `rollback`'s refusal across an unknown revision; a test-only root prefix, `CITAR_DEPLOY_ROOT`, under which the logic runs without root against stubbed `systemctl` and `systemd-run`); `scripts/deploy/bundle.py` (`gh release download`, hashes, `gh attestation verify`, `git archive` of `deploy/` and `env.example`; `--from-dir`).
- **Gates:**
  1. `shellcheck` on `citar-deploy.sh` and `scripts/deploy/`.
  2. `--plan` of `install`, `cutover`, `rollback` (with and without `--restore-backup`), `uncutover` and `prune` against stand-in bundles prints exactly the committed expected texts (`tests/data/deploy/*.txt`, LF-pinned), with and without a slice; a bundle with a changed byte, a wheel of another platform, too little disk and a failing other site in the preflight are each refused before any change, naming why.
  3. **The revision logic, in WSL as `claude`, under `CITAR_DEPLOY_ROOT` with stubs and a real SQLite database:** a failed health check after the revision moved restores the snapshot before the previous release starts, and with the revision unchanged does not; `rollback` across a revision the target does not know is refused without `--restore-backup` and names the deploy's backup; the staged verification's environment holds no live state path.
  4. `bundle.py --from-dir` makes a bundle whose `SHA256SUMS` checks; a tampered wheel is refused; the published path is exercised with a stubbed `gh` (the arguments of `release download` and `attestation verify`).
  5. S1, S2, S4, S8, S9 (gates 1 and 3), S10, S11.

### 5-11: The staging dry run in WSL

- **Depends on:** 5-10b
- **Estimated size:** ~550 bash (`scripts/deploy/staging.sh`) + ~300 Python (`scripts/deploy/staging_check.py`, the probes, the load generator) + RUNBOOK's staging section
- **Scope:** P5.14 with wheels built from this head in WSL: the slice with its probes, `vm.swappiness`, the ballast (`--ballast-mb` from the private log, kept in `ops/p5-notes.md`), the loaded stand-in and the plain one, the 0.1.5 layout, `cutover`, the machine settings at one CPU and 961 MB, the game and the lab under the sandbox, the measurements with the stand-in's response times, the migrating upgrade (`0.1.6-rc.1`), `rollback` across it, the planted migrating failure (`0.1.6-rc.2`), the backup and restore drill, `uncutover`, `cleanup`; the two `-rc` wheels built in the maturin container from bumped scratch clones; the settled `CPUWeight=`/`IOWeight=` and memory figures written into `deploy/citar.service` with the measurement beside them.
- **Gates:**
  1. **The limits bind:** `staging.sh setup` exits 0 with the probes passing (the slice's files read back; the OOM probe killed and counted; the CPU probe within 10% of its quota); **a gate that can fail:** with the slice's `CPUQuota` removed, the CPU probe fails.
  2. `staging.sh run` exits 0, every step's check recorded: the stand-in sites 200 before and after each step; the cutover's health within 60 s; the job host's `ready` reports about 240 MB; the game's 40 rounds and the lab's three games played (the job host under `citar.service`, `RLIMIT_DATA` fallback logged); `oom_kill` 0 in the slice; the stand-in's p95 with a lab game running within twice its idle p95 plus 50 ms; the upgrade, the refused and the accepted rollback, and the second install each healthy with the right version on `/api/meta`; the restore drill reads its accounts; `uncutover` brings 0.1.5 back and it answers.
  3. **Gates that can fail:** the planted `-rc.2` makes `install` restore the snapshot and roll back by itself, exit 1, with `-rc.1` serving at its own revision; a planted release whose server exits before migrating rolls back without a restore.
  4. The measurements (idle, game, lab, pip install, the switch's downtime, seconds a round, the stand-in's response times) in the as-built note with the recommended `MemoryMax`, `MemoryHigh`, host memory figure and weights. If the response-time bound fails even at `CPUWeight=20`, the package stops and reports, and the delegated child cgroup (P5.13.2) becomes a package of its own before 5-12.
  5. `cleanup` leaves S10's WSL items clean and `vm.swappiness` restored.
  6. S2, S4, S5 (RUNBOOK), S10, S11.

### 5-12: The docs pass

- **Depends on:** 5-11
- **Estimated size:** docs (~1,800 lines touched, the server docs from 5-10a and 5-10b included) + `scripts/ci/stale_terms.py` (~120) + tests
- **Scope:** P5.16: every file of its table, `docs/server/DEPLOY.md`, `RUNBOOK.md` (beyond 5-11's staging section) and `VPS.md` included; the CHANGELOG's compatibility item, Added and Fixed; KNOWN_ISSUES; `stale_terms.py` in test.yml's lint; the wiki regenerated (never pushed).
- **Gates:**
  1. Every file in P5.16's table changed, or named in the as-built note as read with nothing to change, with the line that was checked.
  2. `stale_terms.py` passes; each of its phrases planted in a doc fails naming the file and line; the CHANGELOG's history is exempt.
  3. S4, S5 (`mkdocs build --strict`, the wiki committed and unchanged by a second `build_wiki.py`), S1 (the script's tests), S8, S10, S11.

### 5-13: Phase 5 exit

- **Depends on:** 5-12
- **Estimated size:** evidence, scripts' runs, small fixes; "As built in 5-13"
- **Scope:** P5.1.4 measured row by row; the dry run on the pushed `v0.1.6` (P5.18.2); the staging run with the dry run's CI-built linux-x64 wheel and constraints (`gh run download`, then `bundle.py --from-dir`) under the VPS's limits; the bump rehearsal; RELEASING.md walked through in its dry-run forms (`release.py bump` in a scratch clone, `release_checksums.py --from-dir` and `--verify --base-url --pypi-json` over the dry run's `pkg-release-assets`, `managers.sh --plan`, `bundle.py --from-dir`, `citar-deploy --plan`, the image checked with `podman run`); WSL's shared target, clones and podman storage removed and measured (P5.18.3); the Phase 6 handoff in `ops/p5-notes.md`.
- **Gates:**
  1. **The release dry run is green on every platform** (CI-only, dispatched by this package on the pushed head; any fix goes through its merge agent, which dispatches again, three rounds at most): `meta`, test.yml's every leg, and package.yml's every job (the five wheels and their smokes on 3.11 and 3.14, the sdist and its build, the bundle and installer with the silent install, the ten helpers with the glibc check and the manifest with its throwaway signature verified, both images built natively and run, the install scripts on five platforms, `assemble` with one build and one result hash, the package managers), every tag-only job skipped, no environment named, the environments, tags and releases unchanged. The run id, each job's conclusion and duration, and the `pkg-release-preview` contents recorded.
  2. **The staging deploy from the CI-built wheel:** `staging.sh` with a bundle made from the dry run's artifacts for the cutover, and `0.1.7-rc.1` and `0.1.7-rc.2` wheels built locally from this head as 5-11 builds its own, for the upgrade, the rollbacks and the planted failure; every check of 5-11's gates 1 to 3 again, the measurements again, compared with 5-11's.
  3. **The bump rehearsal:** in a scratch clone, `release.py bump 0.1.6`; `cargo xtask develop`; the whole S1 suite and S7's nextest, `golden check` and `xtask check` green there; the clone deleted.
  4. **The checklist's dry runs:** each step of RELEASING.md that has one run in that form, its output recorded; the manifests from the dry run's assets equal the `pkg-release-preview` artifact's; `--verify` passes over the dry run's assets; helpers run only through `gate_run.py helper`.
  5. The key scan (S12); WSL's `~/target/citar-p5`, `~/p5` and podman storage removed with `du` and `df` before and after; S10 before and after the whole package; S1, S2, S4, S8, S11 at the head.
